## Highlight Component Spec Analysis - Task #204

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/spec/components/utility/highlight.md` (163 lines)

---

### 1. **State Machine**
**Stateless tier component** (frontmatter: line 4). No state machine. Pure computation utility with no transitions or events. Renders `<mark>` elements for highlighted chunks via adapter, but the core is stateless — `highlight_chunks()` is a pure function taking Props and Locale, returning `Vec<HighlightChunk>`.

---

### 2. **Public API**

**Props struct** (lines 22–35):
```rust
pub struct Props {
    pub query: Vec<String>,        // multiple queries supported; empty = no highlighting
    pub text: String,              // full text to search within
    pub ignore_case: bool,         // default: true (case-insensitive)
    pub match_strategy: MatchStrategy,
}
```

**MatchStrategy enum** (lines 38–46):
```rust
pub enum MatchStrategy {
    Contains,      // all occurrences
    StartsWith,    // only if text starts with query
    Fuzzy,         // individual character matches
}
```

**HighlightChunk struct** (lines 55–59):
```rust
pub struct HighlightChunk<'a> {
    pub text: &'a str,           // borrowed from Props.text
    pub highlighted: bool,        // true = <mark>, false = <span>
}
```

**Public function** (lines 69–74):
```rust
pub fn highlight_chunks<'a>(props: &'a Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>>
```

---

### 3. **Match Strategies** (Algorithm Details)

The spec provides **intent** but not implementation pseudocode:

- **Contains**: "Highlight all occurrences of the query within the text" (line 40)
- **StartsWith**: "Highlight only if the text starts with the query" (line 42)
- **Fuzzy**: "Highlight individual characters for fuzzy matching" (line 44)

No detailed algorithm is specified in the spec. The spec notes that when `ignore_case=true`, the implementation **must use Unicode case folding** (§1.2, lines 63–68).

---

### 4. **Unicode Handling & ICU4X**

**Case folding requirement** (lines 63–68):

> "When `ignore_case` is `true`, the implementation MUST use Unicode case folding rather than ASCII `to_lowercase()`. If `locale` is provided, use locale-aware case folding via ICU4X `CaseMapper::fold_turkic()` for Turkic locales. Otherwise, use default Unicode case folding (`CaseMapper::fold()`). This ensures correct matching for Turkish dotted-I (İ/i vs I/ı), German eszett (ß/SS), and other locale-specific case mappings."

**ICU4X module**: `CaseMapper` — no module path specified in the spec. (See foundation/03-accessibility.md line 2401 referencing `CaseFolding` for Turkish/Azerbaijani).

**Turkic logic**: Locale-specific check → if Turkic → use `fold_turkic()`, else `fold()`.

---

### 5. **Multi-Query Support & Merging**

Lines 27–28 (Props.query): "Supports a single query string or multiple queries to highlight simultaneously. When multiple queries are provided, all matches from all queries are highlighted; **overlapping matches are merged**."

Lines 72–73 (function comment): "When multiple queries match overlapping regions, the overlapping ranges are merged into a single highlighted chunk."

**Algorithm not specified** — only the result: overlapping ranges collapse into single `highlighted=true` chunks.

---

### 6. **HighlightChunk Structure**

Two fields:
- `pub text: &'a str` — borrowed slice from the original text
- `pub highlighted: bool` — whether this chunk is wrapped in `<mark>`

**No methods** are specified. The chunk is a data-only struct.

---

### 7. **Connect/AttrMap Output (Anatomy)**

This is **NOT** a traditional stateful component with `ConnectApi`/`AttrMap`. Instead (line 51):

> "Highlight uses a **function-based API** that returns text chunks rather than the standard `ConnectApi` / `AttrMap` pattern. The adapter renders each chunk as a DOM element."

**Anatomy** (lines 79–91):

```
Highlight
└── Root        <span>   data-ars-scope="highlight" data-ars-part="root"
    └── Chunk   <mark> | <span>   (×N, per highlight_chunks output)
```

**Parts table** (lines 85–88):

| Part  | Element           | Key Attributes |
|-------|-------------------|----------------|
| Root  | `<span>`          | `data-ars-scope="highlight"`, `data-ars-part="root"` |
| Chunk | `<mark>`/`<span>` | `data-ars-part="highlight-chunk"`, `data-ars-highlighted="true\|false"` |

**Clarification**: The spec calls this "stateless" — no `Api` struct managing state. The adapter directly iterates `highlight_chunks()` output and renders `<mark>` (if `highlighted=true`) or `<span>` (if `false`). See Leptos example (lines 109–119).

---

### 8. **Props: Full List**

| Prop | Type | Default | Notes |
|------|------|---------|-------|
| `query` | `Vec<String>` | — | Multiple queries; empty → no highlighting |
| `text` | `String` | — | Full text to search |
| `ignore_case` | `bool` | `true` | Case-insensitive matching (default per library parity table, line 132) |
| `match_strategy` | `MatchStrategy` | — | Contains, StartsWith, or Fuzzy |

**Fluent builder pattern**: Per foundation/10-component-spec-template.md §4.1.2, stateless components MAY have a builder (setters), but the spec doesn't show them explicitly.

---

### 9. **Foundation/Shared Dependencies**

**Frontmatter** (lines 1–10):

```yaml
component: Highlight
category: utility
tier: stateless
foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]
shared_deps: []
related: []
references:
  ark-ui: Highlight
```

- **foundation_deps**: `architecture`, `accessibility` (no i18n explicitly, but `locale: &Locale` parameter is required)
- **shared_deps**: none
- **related**: none
- **references**: Ark UI Highlight for parity comparison

---

### 10. **Edge Cases**

Lines 72–73 (function comment): "When queries are empty or all empty strings, returns the full text as a single non-highlighted chunk."

Other edge cases **not explicitly specified** in the spec:
- Empty text + non-empty query → ? (presumably returns nothing or empty vec)
- Overlapping fuzzy character matches → merged per §5
- Adjacent highlighted chunks → presumably stay separate unless overlapping from different queries

---

### 11. **Accessibility (§3)**

**§3.1 ARIA** (lines 95–98):
- `<mark>` is semantic; screen readers announce "highlighted"
- No explicit ARIA attributes needed
- Each chunk gets `data-ars-part="highlight-chunk"` and `data-ars-highlighted="true|false"`

**§3.2 Fuzzy verbosity warning** (lines 99–99):
- Fuzzy matching can generate many small `<mark>` elements
- Adapters SHOULD consolidate adjacent highlighted chunks OR provide summary announcement

**§3.3 BiDi text** (lines 101–103):
- Adapter SHOULD use `dir="auto"` on root
- Consider `unicode-bidi: isolate` on chunk elements

---

### 12. **Template Conformance Check**

Per foundation/10-component-spec-template.md §3.1:

Stateless tier requires:
- **§1.1 Props** ✓ (lines 18–35)
- **§1.2 Connect / API** ✓ (lines 49–74)
- **§2 Anatomy** ✓ (lines 77–90)
- **§3 Accessibility** ✓ (lines 92–103)
- **§N Library Parity** ✓ (lines 122–163)

Missing:
- §N **Internationalization** subsection — not formally titled but `locale` parameter and Turkic case folding are documented inline (lines 63–68)
- §N **Layering** section — not included; stateless components do not typically need explicit layering

---

**Summary**: Highlight is a **pure computation utility**. It returns `Vec<HighlightChunk>` from a pure function. Adapters render chunks. Multi-query overlaps are merged. Unicode case folding is mandatory when `ignore_case=true`, with locale-aware Turkic folding support via ICU4X `CaseMapper`. No state machine, no state machine section, no ConnectApi—just Props → function → chunks → DOM.
```

## Summary: ICU4X CaseMapper Integration for Task #204 (Highlight Component)

### 1. **CaseMapper Usage in Codebase**

**Files with ICU4X case mapping:**
- `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/crates/ars-i18n/src/case.rs` — Core API (`to_uppercase`, `to_lowercase`)
- `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/crates/ars-collections/src/typeahead.rs` — Uses `CaseMapper` for Typeahead component
- `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/spec/foundation/04-internationalization.md` — Spec documentation

### 2. **Public API Export in `ars-i18n`**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/crates/ars-i18n/src/lib.rs` (lines 56-57)

```rust
#[cfg(any(feature = "icu4x", feature = "web-intl"))]
pub use case::{to_lowercase, to_uppercase};
```

**Key point:** `CaseMapper` is NOT re-exported. Only `to_uppercase()` and `to_lowercase()` functions are public. For the Highlight component, you should call these locale-aware helpers directly rather than constructing `CaseMapper` manually.

### 3. **ICU4X Version & API**

**Workspace Cargo.toml** (`crates/ars-i18n/Cargo.toml`, line 14):
```toml
icu = { version = "2.2", default-features = false }
```

**ICU4X 2.x API (from actual usage in `case.rs` lines 29-31, 71-73):**
```rust
// Method signature:
CaseMapper::new()
    .uppercase_to_string(text, locale.language_identifier())
    .into_owned()

CaseMapper::new()
    .lowercase_to_string(text, locale.language_identifier())
    .into_owned()
```

**Important:** The methods take `&LanguageIdentifier` (not `&DataLocale`). Retrieve it via `locale.language_identifier()` on the `Locale` wrapper (defined in `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/crates/ars-i18n/src/locale.rs`, lines 65-67).

### 4. **Highlight Component Spec Requirements**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/spec/components/utility/highlight.md` (lines 63-74)

The spec REQUIRES Unicode case folding with locale awareness:

```rust
/// When `ignore_case` is `true`, the implementation MUST use Unicode case folding
/// rather than ASCII `to_lowercase()`. If `locale` is provided, use locale-aware
/// case folding via ICU4X `CaseMapper::fold_turkic()` for Turkic locales. Otherwise,
/// use default Unicode case folding (`CaseMapper::fold()`).
pub fn highlight_chunks<'a>(props: &'a Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>>
```

**Critical issue:** The spec references methods `fold_turkic()` and `fold()` that do NOT exist in ICU4X 2.x. The actual methods are:
- `uppercase_to_string()` / `lowercase_to_string()` (case transformation, not folding)

**ACTION NEEDED:** Update the spec to use correct ICU4X 2.x method names. The implementation should use `to_lowercase()` from `ars-i18n` for case-insensitive matching.

### 5. **Locale Parameter Handling**

The Highlight component signature in the spec (line 69) requires:
```rust
pub fn highlight_chunks<'a>(props: &'a Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>>
```

**Props structure** (lines 21-35) does NOT include locale — it's a separate parameter. The component must receive the locale from:
1. Component prop (if added)
2. Context via `use_locale()` from `ArsProvider` (per section 1.1.1 of the i18n spec)
3. Fallback to `Locale::parse("en-US")`

### 6. **Typeahead Usage Pattern (Reference Implementation)**

**File:** `/Users/ericson/.t3/worktrees/ars-ui/t3code-7f73cfa1/crates/ars-collections/src/typeahead.rs` (lines 244-246)

```rust
let case_mapper = icu::casemap::CaseMapper::new();
let langid = locale.language_identifier();
// ... case_mapper used for case-insensitive filtering
```

**Pattern to follow:** Construct `CaseMapper::new()` (it's `Copy` and zero-cost), then pass `locale.language_identifier()` to methods.

### 7. **Locale-Specific Case Mapping Tests**

The `case.rs` test suite (lines 125-177) validates Turkish, German, Lithuanian, and Greek locales:
- Turkish `İ` (dotted capital I) ↔ `i`
- Turkish `I` ↔ `ı` (dotless lowercase i)
- German `ß` → `SS` (eszett expansion)
- Greek σ/Σ (final sigma rules)

**For Highlight:** The component should pass these same locale-specific tests when `ignore_case=true`.

---

**Bottom line:** Use `ars_i18n::to_lowercase(text, &locale)` for case-insensitive comparison. Do NOT use ICU4X methods directly; the crate abstracts them. Update the spec to remove references to non-existent `fold_turkic()`/`fold()` methods.
```

## Summary: Framework-Agnostic Utility Component Patterns for Highlight (#204)

Based on my thorough exploration of the `ars-components` crate, here's the established pattern for implementing the stateless `Highlight` utility component:

### 1. Utility Module Structure

**Location:** `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/`

**Existing utility components:**
- Single-file components: `separator.rs`, `visually_hidden.rs`, `keyboard.rs`, `focus_ring.rs`, `landmark.rs`, `dismissable.rs`, `button.rs`, `as_child.rs`, `client_only.rs`, `error_boundary.rs`, `form_submit.rs`
- Directory-based (with submodules): `field/`, `fieldset/`, `form/`, `heading/`

**For Highlight:** Single file (`highlight.rs`) is appropriate since the spec shows a stateless, function-based API (not the typical `ConnectApi` pattern).

---

### 2. Module Anatomy Pattern (from Separator & VisuallyHidden)

**Single-file structure:**
```
separator.rs:
├── Documentation (//! docstring)
├── Imports (alloc, ars_core, ars_i18n)
├── Props struct
│   ├── Fields (id, orientation, decorative, etc.)
│   ├── Default derive
│   ├── HasId derive
│   ├── impl Props { new(), builder setters }
├── Part enum (ComponentPart derive)
├── Api struct (props field)
│   ├── new() constructor
│   ├── props() accessor
│   ├── Field accessors (id, orientation, decorative)
│   ├── root_attrs() → AttrMap  [or similar output methods]
├── impl ConnectApi for Api
│   └── part_attrs(part) dispatcher
└── #[cfg(test)] mod tests
    ├── Helper functions (default_props, decorative_props, etc.)
    ├── Props tests (default, builder, HasId, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)
    ├── Api tests (accessors, ConnectApi dispatch)
    ├── Attribute generation tests (cross-branch inequality checks)
    └── Snapshot tests (assert_snapshot! macros)
```

---

### 3. Public Type Patterns

**Standard for stateless utilities:**
- `Props`: Derives `Clone`, `Debug`, `Default`, `PartialEq`, `HasId`
  - Immutable fields
  - Builder setters (const where possible)
  - `new()` entry point equivalent to `Default::default()`

- `Part`: Derives `ComponentPart` (custom derive)
  - `#[scope = "component-name"]` attribute
  - Single `Root` variant (typical)
  - Emits `data-ars-scope` and `data-ars-part` attributes via `Part::Root.data_attrs()`

- `Api`: Derives `Clone`, `Debug`
  - Owns `Props` field (immutable)
  - `new(props: Props) → Self`
  - `props() → &Props` (escape hatch)
  - Accessors for each prop field
  - Core computation method (e.g., `root_attrs() → AttrMap`)
  - Implements `ConnectApi` trait

**For Highlight (different pattern per spec):**
Since the spec defines a function-based API (`highlight_chunks`) returning `Vec<HighlightChunk>` instead of `AttrMap`, the module will be simpler:
- `Props` struct (standard)
- `MatchStrategy` enum
- `HighlightChunk<'a>` struct (returned by function, NOT wrapped in Api)
- `highlight_chunks(props: &Props, locale: &Locale) → Vec<HighlightChunk>` function
- No `Part` enum or `ConnectApi` (breaking the pattern intentionally per spec)

---

### 4. Snapshot Test Pattern

**Location:** `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/` (auto-generated by `insta`)

**File naming:** `ars_components__utility__{component}__tests__{test_name}.snap`

**Structure (example from separator):**
```yaml
---
source: crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs
expression: "snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs())"
---
AttrMap {
    attrs: [
        (Data("ars-orientation"), String("horizontal")),
        (Data("ars-part"), String("root")),
        (Data("ars-scope"), String("separator")),
        (Aria(Orientation), String("horizontal")),
        (Role, String("separator")),
    ],
    styles: [],
}
```

**Test pattern in code:**
```rust
#[test]
fn separator_root_horizontal_snapshot() {
    assert_snapshot!(
        "separator_root_horizontal",
        snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs())
    );
}

fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
    format!("{attrs:#?}")
}
```

**For Highlight:** Snapshots would test `highlight_chunks()` output directly:
```rust
#[test]
fn highlight_chunks_contains_en_us_snapshot() {
    assert_snapshot!(
        "highlight_chunks_contains_en_us",
        format!("{:#?}", highlight_chunks(&props, &locale))
    );
}
```

### 5. Module Registration

**In `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs`:**
```rust
/// `Highlight` component (utility text splitting for search highlighting).
pub mod highlight;
```

**In `/crates/ars-components/src/lib.rs`:**
Automatically exposed via `pub use ars_components::utility::*` or explicit re-export.

---

### 6. Cargo Dependencies: ars-i18n CaseMapper

**File:** `/crates/ars-components/Cargo.toml` (lines 22-28)
```toml
[dependencies]
ars-a11y         = { path = "../ars-a11y", default-features = false }
ars-collections  = { path = "../ars-collections", default-features = false }
ars-core         = { path = "../ars-core", default-features = false }
ars-forms        = { path = "../ars-forms", default-features = false }
ars-i18n         = { path = "../ars-i18n", default-features = false }  ← Already present
ars-interactions = { path = "../ars-interactions", default-features = false }
```

**CaseMapper availability in ars-i18n:**
- **File:** `/crates/ars-i18n/src/case.rs`
- **Exposed at:** `ars_i18n::{to_uppercase, to_lowercase}` (lines 56-57 of lib.rs)
- **Feature-gated:** Only available with `icu4x` or `web-intl` features
- **Implementation:** Uses `icu::casemap::CaseMapper` (ICU4X 2.x):
  ```rust
  #[cfg(feature = "icu4x")]
  pub fn to_uppercase(text: &str, locale: &Locale) -> String {
      CaseMapper::new()
          .uppercase_to_string(text, locale.language_identifier())
          .into_owned()
  }
  ```

**For Highlight's case folding:** The spec (line 63-68) calls for `CaseMapper::fold()` (default Unicode) or `fold_turkic()` (Turkic locales). These methods exist in ICU4X 2.x but aren't currently wrapped by ars-i18n. You'll either:
1. Add `fold()` / `fold_turkic()` wrappers to ars-i18n/src/case.rs, OR
2. Import `icu::casemap::CaseMapper` directly in highlight.rs (acceptable since ars-components already depends on ICU4X transitively via ars-i18n)

---

### 7. Test Directory Structure

**Unit tests:** Inline in `.rs` files (all modules use `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`)
- No external `crates/ars-components/tests/` subdirectories for utility components

**Property-based tests:** `/crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/utility.rs`
- Tests arbitrary Props generation and state transitions
- Example: `arb_separator_props()` strategy

**Snapshot tests:** Auto-stored in `src/utility/snapshots/` alongside each component
- Insta-managed (run `cargo insta accept` to update)

**None found.** Grep search for "fuzzy", "highlight", "case_fold" across the crate revealed:

- This is a new addition to the utility component family

### Key Takeaways for Implementation

1. **File:** Create `/crates/ars-components/src/utility/highlight.rs` (single file)

2. **Imports:**
   ```rust
   use alloc::string::String;
   use alloc::vec::Vec;
   use ars_i18n::Locale;
   // Import CaseMapper or use ars-i18n case helpers
   ```

3. **Core API (function-based, NOT ConnectApi):**
   ```rust
   pub struct Props { query, text, ignore_case, match_strategy }
   pub enum MatchStrategy { Contains, StartsWith, Fuzzy }
   pub struct HighlightChunk<'a> { text: &'a str, highlighted: bool }
   pub fn highlight_chunks(props: &Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk>
   ```

4. **Test pattern:**
   - Props round-trip tests
   - Snapshot tests for chunk output across match strategies
   - Property tests in `/tests/proptest_state_machines/utility.rs`

5. **No ConnectApi:** Unlike separator/visually_hidden, Highlight returns chunks for the adapter to render, not attributes.

6. **Case folding strategy:** Use ICU4X `CaseMapper` methods (either wrapped via ars-i18n or directly imported) for Unicode case folding and locale-aware Turkic handling per spec §1.2 line 63-68.
```

1	---
2	component: Highlight
3	category: utility
4	tier: stateless
5	foundation_deps: [architecture, accessibility]
6	shared_deps: []
7	related: []
8	references:

10	---
11	
12	# Highlight
13	
14	`Highlight` is a pure computation utility that splits text into highlighted and non-highlighted chunks based on a search query. It has no state machine — the adapter renders `<mark>` elements for highlighted chunks.
15	
16	## 1. API
17	
18	### 1.1 Props
19	
20	```rust
21	/// Props for the `Highlight` component.
22	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
23	pub struct Props {
24	    /// The search queries to highlight. Supports a single query string or
25	    /// multiple queries to highlight simultaneously (e.g., multiple search
26	    /// terms). When multiple queries are provided, all matches from all
27	    /// queries are highlighted; overlapping matches are merged.
28	    pub query: Vec<String>,
29	    /// The full text to search within.
30	    pub text: String,
31	    /// Case-insensitive matching (default: true).
32	    pub ignore_case: bool,
33	    /// Matching strategy.
34	    pub match_strategy: MatchStrategy,
35	}
36	
37	/// The strategy to use for matching the query within the text.
38	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
39	pub enum MatchStrategy {
40	    /// Highlight all occurrences of the query within the text.
41	    Contains,
42	    /// Highlight only if the text starts with the query.
43	    StartsWith,
44	    /// Highlight individual characters for fuzzy matching.
45	    Fuzzy,
46	}
47	```
48	
49	### 1.2 Connect / API
50	
51	`Highlight` uses a function-based API that returns text chunks rather than the standard `ConnectApi` / `AttrMap` pattern. The adapter renders each chunk as a DOM element.
52	
53	```rust
54	/// A chunk of text that has been highlighted or not.
55	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
56	pub struct HighlightChunk<'a> {
57	    pub text: &'a str,
58	    pub highlighted: bool,
59	}
60	
61	/// Returns the text split into alternating highlighted/non-highlighted segments.
62	///
63	/// When `ignore_case` is `true`, the implementation MUST use Unicode case folding
64	/// rather than ASCII `to_lowercase()`. If `locale` is provided, use locale-aware
65	/// case folding via ICU4X `CaseMapper::fold_turkic()` for Turkic locales. Otherwise,
66	/// use default Unicode case folding (`CaseMapper::fold()`). This ensures correct
67	/// matching for Turkish dotted-I (İ/i vs I/ı), German eszett (ß/SS), and other
68	/// locale-specific case mappings.
69	pub fn highlight_chunks<'a>(props: &'a Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>> {
70	    // Returns the text split into alternating highlighted/non-highlighted segments.
71	    // When queries are empty or all empty strings, returns the full text as a single
72	    // non-highlighted chunk. When multiple queries match overlapping regions, the
73	    // overlapping ranges are merged into a single highlighted chunk.
74	}
75	```
76	
77	## 2. Anatomy
78	
79	```text
80	Highlight
81	└── Root        <span>   data-ars-scope="highlight" data-ars-part="root"
82	    └── Chunk   <mark> | <span>   (×N, per highlight_chunks output)
83	```
84	
85	| Part  | Element           | Key Attributes                                                          |
86	| ----- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
87	| Root  | `<span>`          | `data-ars-scope="highlight"`, `data-ars-part="root"`                    |
88	| Chunk | `<mark>`/`<span>` | `data-ars-part="highlight-chunk"`, `data-ars-highlighted="true\|false"` |
89	
90	Adapters render each `HighlightChunk` as a `<mark>` (when `highlighted == true`) or `<span>` (when `highlighted == false`).
91	
92	## 3. Accessibility
93	
94	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
95	
96	- `<mark>` elements are used for highlighted text. Screen readers announce `<mark>` content as "highlighted" in most browser/AT combinations.
97	- No ARIA attributes needed — the semantic `<mark>` element conveys the highlighting.
98	- For custom styling, adapters set `data-ars-part="highlight-chunk"` and `data-ars-highlighted="true|false"` on each chunk.
99	- When using fuzzy matching (`MatchStrategy::Fuzzy`), many small `<mark>` elements may be generated. Each `<mark>` is announced as 'highlighted' by screen readers, which can create excessive verbosity. For fuzzy matches with many small highlights, adapters SHOULD consolidate adjacent highlighted chunks into a single `<mark>` element, or provide a summary announcement (e.g., 'N characters matched') instead of wrapping each character individually.
100	
101	### 3.2 Bidirectional Text
102	
103	When highlighting text in mixed-direction content (e.g., English query in Arabic text), chunk boundaries may disrupt BiDi ordering. The adapter SHOULD set `dir="auto"` on the root container element, and consider applying `unicode-bidi: isolate` on individual `<mark>` and `<span>` elements to prevent chunk boundaries from affecting text reordering.
104	
105	## 4. Adapter Rendering
106	
107	```rust
108	// Leptos example:
109	view! {
110	    <span data-ars-scope="highlight" data-ars-part="root">
111	        {highlight_chunks(&props).into_iter().map(|chunk| {
112	            if chunk.highlighted {
113	                view! { <mark>{chunk.text}</mark> }
114	            } else {
115	                view! { <span>{chunk.text}</span> }
116	            }
117	        }).collect_view()}
118	    </span>
119	}
120	```
121	
122	## 5. Library Parity
123	
124	> Compared against: Ark UI (`Highlight`).
125	
126	### 5.1 Props
127	
128	| Feature        | ars-ui                          | Ark UI                      | Notes                                         |
129	| -------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
130	| Query          | `query: Vec<String>`            | `query: string \| string[]` | Both support multiple queries                 |
131	| Text           | `text`                          | `text`                      | Both libraries                                |
132	| Ignore case    | `ignore_case`                   | `ignoreCase`                | Both libraries                                |
133	| Match strategy | `match_strategy: MatchStrategy` | `exactMatch`, `matchAll`    | ars-ui uses enum; Ark uses boolean flags      |
134	| Locale         | `locale`                        | --                          | ars-ui addition for locale-aware case folding |
135	
136	**Gaps:** None.
137	
138	### 5.2 Anatomy
139	
140	| Part  | ars-ui              | Ark UI              | Notes                                        |
141	| ----- | ------------------- | ------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
142	| Root  | `Root`              | (inline rendering)  | Both produce chunks for rendering            |
143	| Chunk | `<mark>` / `<span>` | `<mark>` / `<span>` | Both libraries use mark for highlighted text |
144	
145	**Gaps:** None.
146	
147	### 5.3 Features
148	
149	| Feature              | ars-ui                            | Ark UI |
150	| -------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------ |
151	| Multi-query          | Yes                               | Yes    |
152	| Case-insensitive     | Yes                               | Yes    |
153	| Fuzzy matching       | Yes (`MatchStrategy::Fuzzy`)      | --     |
154	| StartsWith matching  | Yes (`MatchStrategy::StartsWith`) | --     |
155	| Locale-aware folding | Yes                               | --     |
156	
157	**Gaps:** None.
158	
159	### 5.4 Summary
160	
161	- **Overall:** Full parity -- ars-ui is a superset.
162	- **Divergences:** ars-ui uses `MatchStrategy` enum (Contains/StartsWith/Fuzzy) while Ark uses `exactMatch`/`matchAll` booleans. ars-ui adds locale-aware Unicode case folding and fuzzy matching.
163	- **Recommended additions:** None.
164	
```

1	//! `Separator` component machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! `Separator` is a stateless attribute mapper that renders a horizontal or
4	//! vertical dividing line. It has no state machine — the framework-agnostic
5	//! core consists solely of `Props`, `Part`, and `Api`, which produces the
6	//! appropriate ARIA role and orientation attributes for the root element.
7	//!
8	//! See `spec/components/utility/separator.md` for the authoritative contract.
9	
10	use alloc::string::String;
11	
12	use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr};
13	use ars_i18n::Orientation;
14	
15	/// Props for the `Separator` component.
16	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, HasId)]
17	pub struct Props {
18	    /// Component instance ID.
19	    pub id: String,
20	
21	    /// The orientation of the separator. Defaults to `Orientation::Horizontal`
22	    /// via `Orientation`'s own `Default` impl.
23	    pub orientation: Orientation,
24	
25	    /// Whether the separator is purely decorative and hidden from the
26	    /// accessibility tree.
27	    pub decorative: bool,
28	}
29	
30	impl Props {
31	    /// Returns fresh [`Props`] with the documented defaults — equivalent
32	    /// to [`Default::default`], offered as the entry point for the
33	    /// builder chain.
34	    #[must_use]
35	    pub fn new() -> Self {
36	        Self::default()
37	    }
38	
39	    /// Sets the component instance ID.
40	    #[must_use]
41	    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
42	        self.id = id.into();
43	        self
44	    }
45	
46	    /// Sets the layout orientation of the separator.
47	    #[must_use]
48	    pub const fn orientation(mut self, value: Orientation) -> Self {
49	        self.orientation = value;
50	        self
51	    }
52	
53	    /// Sets whether the separator is purely decorative (hidden from the
54	    /// accessibility tree).
55	    #[must_use]
56	    pub const fn decorative(mut self, value: bool) -> Self {
57	        self.decorative = value;
58	        self
59	    }
60	}
61	
62	/// DOM parts of the `Separator` component.
63	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
64	#[scope = "separator"]
65	pub enum Part {
66	    /// The root element. See spec §2.1 for adapter element-type selection
67	    /// (`<hr>` for content separators, `<div>` for menu/toolbar/listbox).
68	    Root,
69	}
70	
71	/// The API for the `Separator` component.
72	///
73	/// Constructed via [`Api::new`] from [`Props`] and queried via
74	/// [`Api::root_attrs`] or the [`ConnectApi`] dispatch.
75	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
76	pub struct Api {
77	    props: Props,
78	}
79	
80	impl Api {
81	    /// Creates a new `Api` instance from the given props.
82	    #[must_use]
83	    pub const fn new(props: Props) -> Self {
84	        Self { props }
85	    }
86	
87	    /// Returns a reference to the underlying [`Props`].
88	    ///
89	    /// Adapters typically read individual fields through the dedicated
90	    /// accessors (`id`, `orientation`, `decorative`); this method is the
91	    /// escape hatch for when the full struct is needed (e.g., to clone it
92	    /// into a fresh [`Api`] for a re-render).
93	    #[must_use]
94	    pub const fn props(&self) -> &Props {
95	        &self.props
96	    }
97	
98	    /// Returns the component's instance ID.
99	    #[must_use]
100	    pub fn id(&self) -> &str {
101	        &self.props.id
102	    }
103	
104	    /// Returns the layout orientation of the separator.
105	    #[must_use]
106	    pub const fn orientation(&self) -> Orientation {
107	        self.props.orientation
108	    }
109	
110	    /// Returns whether the separator is purely decorative.
111	    #[must_use]
112	    pub const fn decorative(&self) -> bool {
113	        self.props.decorative
114	    }
115	
116	    /// Returns the attributes for the root element.
117	    ///
118	    /// Semantic separators get `role="separator"` plus `aria-orientation`
119	    /// matching the layout axis, and `data-ars-orientation` for styling.
120	    /// Decorative separators get `role="none"` (the modern WAI-ARIA 1.2
121	    /// preferred form, synonymous with `role="presentation"`); they omit
122	    /// `aria-orientation` and the `data-ars-orientation` styling hook,
123	    /// because a decorative separator is invisible to assistive technology
124	    /// and component-managed orientation styling is not applied.
125	    #[must_use]
126	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
127	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
128	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
129	
130	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
131	
132	        if self.props.decorative {
133	            // Decorative separators are removed from the accessibility tree
134	            // via `role="none"`. No `aria-hidden` (redundant for an element
135	            // with no children) and no `data-ars-orientation` (decorative
136	            // separators do not participate in component orientation styling).
137	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "none");
138	        } else {
139	            let orientation_str = match self.props.orientation {
140	                Orientation::Horizontal => "horizontal",
141	                Orientation::Vertical => "vertical",
142	            };
143	
144	            attrs
145	                .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation"), orientation_str)
146	                .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "separator")
147	                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Orientation), orientation_str);
148	        }
149	
150	        attrs
151	    }
152	}
153	
154	impl ConnectApi for Api {
155	    type Part = Part;
156	
157	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
158	        match part {
159	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
160	        }
161	    }
162	}

287	        // Direct assertion on the empty-id path through `Api::id()`.
288	        assert_eq!(Api::new(Props::default()).id(), "");
289	    }
290	
291	    // ── Api accessors ──────────────────────────────────────────────
292	
293	    #[test]
294	    fn api_exposes_props_fields() {
295	        let original = Props {
296	            id: String::from("sep-7"),
297	            orientation: Orientation::Vertical,
298	            decorative: true,
299	        };
300	
301	        let api = Api::new(original.clone());
302	
303	        assert_eq!(api.id(), "sep-7");
304	        assert_eq!(api.orientation(), Orientation::Vertical);
305	        assert!(api.decorative());
306	        assert_eq!(api.props(), &original);
307	
308	        let default = Api::new(horizontal());
309	
310	        assert!(!default.decorative());
311	    }
312	
313	    // ── Connect / API ──────────────────────────────────────────────
314	
315	    #[test]
316	    fn part_attrs_dispatches_root() {
317	        let api = Api::new(horizontal());
318	
319	        let attrs = api.part_attrs(Part::Root);
320	
321	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-scope")), Some("separator"));
322	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-part")), Some("root"));
323	    }
324	
325	    #[test]
326	    fn part_attrs_root_equals_root_attrs() {
327	        // The `ConnectApi` dispatch must produce exactly what the inherent
328	        // `root_attrs` method produces. Asserted across every output-affecting
329	        // prop combination (orientation × decorative).
330	        for props in [
331	            horizontal(),
332	            vertical(),
333	            decorative_horizontal(),
334	            decorative_vertical(),
335	        ] {
336	            let api = Api::new(props);
337	
338	            assert_eq!(api.part_attrs(Part::Root), api.root_attrs());
339	        }
340	    }
341	
342	    #[test]
343	    fn root_horizontal_has_separator_role_and_orientation() {
344	        let attrs = Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs();
345	
346	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("separator"));
347	        assert_eq!(
348	            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Orientation)),
349	            Some("horizontal")
350	        );
351	        assert_eq!(
352	            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation")),
353	            Some("horizontal")
354	        );
355	    }
356	
357	    #[test]
358	    fn root_vertical_has_aria_orientation_vertical() {
359	        let attrs = Api::new(vertical()).root_attrs();
360	
361	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("separator"));
362	        assert_eq!(
363	            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Orientation)),
364	            Some("vertical")
365	        );
366	        assert_eq!(
367	            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation")),
368	            Some("vertical")
369	        );
370	    }
371	
372	    #[test]
373	    fn decorative_uses_none_role_and_omits_aria_attrs() {
374	        let attrs = Api::new(decorative_horizontal()).root_attrs();
375	
376	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("none"));
377	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden)), None);
378	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Orientation)), None);
379	        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data("ars-orientation")), None);
380	    }
381	
382	    #[test]
383	    fn decorative_orientation_does_not_change_root_attrs() {
384	        // Defensive regression test: orientation is invisible to AT for
385	        // decorative separators, and decorative separators do not opt into
386	        // component orientation styling. Both decorative variants must
387	        // produce the same `AttrMap`.
388	        let h = Api::new(decorative_horizontal()).root_attrs();
389	        let v = Api::new(decorative_vertical()).root_attrs();
390	
391	        assert_eq!(h, v);
392	    }
393	
394	    #[test]
395	    fn decorative_and_semantic_branches_produce_different_attrs() {
396	        // Defensive cross-branch inequality: a regression that conflates
397	        // the two role tokens (e.g. accidentally emitting `role="separator"`
398	        // for decorative, or reusing the semantic AttrMap entirely) must
399	        // not pass silently.
400	        let semantic = Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs();
401	        let decorative = Api::new(decorative_horizontal()).root_attrs();
402	
403	        assert_ne!(semantic, decorative);
404	    }
405	
406	    #[test]
407	    fn horizontal_and_vertical_semantic_branches_produce_different_attrs() {
408	        // Defensive cross-axis inequality: orientation MUST change the
409	        // `aria-orientation` and `data-ars-orientation` values on the
410	        // semantic path. Catches a regression where the orientation
411	        // mapping accidentally emits the same value for both axes.
412	        let h = Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs();
413	        let v = Api::new(vertical()).root_attrs();
414	
415	        assert_ne!(h, v);
416	    }
417	
418	    // ── Snapshots ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
419	
420	    #[test]
421	    fn separator_root_horizontal_snapshot() {
422	        assert_snapshot!(
423	            "separator_root_horizontal",
424	            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(horizontal()).root_attrs())
425	        );
426	    }
427	
428	    #[test]
429	    fn separator_root_vertical_snapshot() {
430	        assert_snapshot!(
431	            "separator_root_vertical",
432	            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(vertical()).root_attrs())
433	        );
434	    }
435	
436	    #[test]
437	    fn separator_root_decorative_snapshot() {
438	        // Decorative separators emit the same AttrMap regardless of
439	        // orientation, so a single snapshot covers both decorative branches.
440	        assert_snapshot!(
441	            "separator_root_decorative",
442	            snapshot_attrs(&Api::new(decorative_horizontal()).root_attrs())
443	        );
444	    }
445	}

1	//! Locale-aware text case transformation helpers.
2	//!
3	//! Rust's built-in Unicode case conversion is locale-independent, which means
4	//! it misses language-sensitive mappings such as Turkish dotted and dotless I
5	//! and Greek final sigma handling. These helpers delegate to ICU4X
6	//! [`CaseMapper`](icu::casemap::CaseMapper) or the browser's locale-aware
7	//! string APIs so components can apply the spec-defined behavior consistently
8	//! across backends.
9	
10	use alloc::string::String;
11	
12	#[cfg(feature = "icu4x")]
13	use icu::casemap::CaseMapper;
14	#[cfg(all(feature = "web-intl", target_arch = "wasm32", not(feature = "icu4x")))]
15	use js_sys::JsString;
16	
17	use crate::Locale;
18	
19	/// Locale-aware uppercase transformation.
20	///
21	/// Delegates to ICU4X [`CaseMapper`] so locale-specific mappings such as
22	/// Turkish dotted capital I are preserved. Under the wasm `web-intl` backend,
23	/// this delegates to browser `String.prototype.toLocaleUpperCase()`. On
24	/// non-wasm builds with `web-intl`, it falls back to Unicode case conversion so
25	/// the public API remains available in feature-matrix builds.
26	#[must_use]
27	#[cfg(feature = "icu4x")]
28	pub fn to_uppercase(text: &str, locale: &Locale) -> String {
29	    CaseMapper::new()
30	        .uppercase_to_string(text, locale.language_identifier())
31	        .into_owned()
32	}
33	
34	/// Locale-aware uppercase transformation.
35	///
36	/// Delegates to browser `String.prototype.toLocaleUpperCase()` on wasm
37	/// `web-intl` builds.
38	#[must_use]
39	#[cfg(all(feature = "web-intl", target_arch = "wasm32", not(feature = "icu4x")))]
40	pub fn to_uppercase(text: &str, locale: &Locale) -> String {
41	    let locale_tag = locale.to_bcp47();
42	    String::from(JsString::from(text).to_locale_upper_case(Some(locale_tag.as_str())))
43	}
44	
45	/// Locale-aware uppercase transformation.
46	///
47	/// Non-wasm `web-intl` builds cannot access the browser `Intl` APIs, so this
48	/// falls back to Rust's Unicode case mapping while preserving the shared public
49	/// API surface.
50	#[must_use]
51	#[cfg(all(
52	    feature = "web-intl",
53	    not(target_arch = "wasm32"),
54	    not(feature = "icu4x")
55	))]
56	pub fn to_uppercase(text: &str, _locale: &Locale) -> String {
57	    text.to_uppercase()
58	}
59	
60	/// Locale-aware lowercase transformation.
61	///
62	/// Delegates to ICU4X [`CaseMapper`] so locale-specific mappings such as
63	/// Turkish dotless i and Greek final sigma are preserved. Under the wasm
64	/// `web-intl` backend, this delegates to browser
65	/// `String.prototype.toLocaleLowerCase()`. On non-wasm builds with `web-intl`,
66	/// it falls back to Unicode case conversion so the public API remains
67	/// available in feature-matrix builds.
68	#[must_use]
69	#[cfg(feature = "icu4x")]
70	pub fn to_lowercase(text: &str, locale: &Locale) -> String {
71	    CaseMapper::new()
72	        .lowercase_to_string(text, locale.language_identifier())
73	        .into_owned()
74	}
75	
76	/// Locale-aware lowercase transformation.
77	///
78	/// Delegates to browser `String.prototype.toLocaleLowerCase()` on wasm
79	/// `web-intl` builds.
80	#[must_use]
81	#[cfg(all(feature = "web-intl", target_arch = "wasm32", not(feature = "icu4x")))]
82	pub fn to_lowercase(text: &str, locale: &Locale) -> String {
83	    let locale_tag = locale.to_bcp47();
84	    String::from(JsString::from(text).to_locale_lower_case(Some(locale_tag.as_str())))
85	}
86	
87	/// Locale-aware lowercase transformation.
88	///
89	/// Non-wasm `web-intl` builds cannot access the browser `Intl` APIs, so this
90	/// falls back to Rust's Unicode case mapping while preserving the shared public
91	/// API surface.
92	#[must_use]
93	#[cfg(all(
94	    feature = "web-intl",
95	    not(target_arch = "wasm32"),
96	    not(feature = "icu4x")
97	))]
98	pub fn to_lowercase(text: &str, _locale: &Locale) -> String {
99	    text.to_lowercase()
100	}

1	//! Internationalization types for locale, number formatting, locale-aware
2	//! string sorting, text direction, layout orientation, plural rules,
3	//! locale-aware case mapping, and logical-to-physical layout geometry.
4	//!
5	//! This crate provides the core i18n primitives shared across all ars-ui components:
6	//! a BCP 47 [`Locale`] wrapper, a locale-aware [`number::Formatter`], a
7	//! [`Direction`] enum for LTR/RTL text flow, a [`StringCollator`] for
8	//! locale-aware sorting, an [`Orientation`] enum for horizontal/vertical
9	//! layout axes, RTL-aware layout geometry types ([`LogicalSide`],
10	//! [`PhysicalSide`], [`LogicalRect`], [`PhysicalRect`]), plural and ordinal
11	//! helpers, locale-aware [`to_uppercase`] and [`to_lowercase`] helpers, and
12	//! the [`IntlBackend`] trait for calendar/locale data abstraction.
13	
14	#![cfg_attr(not(feature = "std"), no_std)]
15	
16	#[cfg(all(feature = "icu4x", feature = "web-intl"))]
17	compile_error!("features `icu4x` and `web-intl` are mutually exclusive");
18	
19	extern crate alloc;
20	
21	use alloc::string::{String, ToString};
22	use core::num::NonZero;
23	
24	mod bidi;
25	pub mod calendar;

228	    /// Find the first item whose `text_value` starts with `search` using
229	    /// locale-aware case folding via ICU4X `CaseMapper`, beginning the search
230	    /// from the item *after* `current_focus` (single-char, cycling) or *at*
231	    /// `current_focus` (multi-char, refining).
232	    ///
233	    /// Single-character searches wrap; multi-character searches do not (they
234	    /// stay within the current alphabetical run to avoid disorienting jumps).
235	    #[cfg(feature = "i18n")]
236	    fn find_match<T, C: Collection<T>>(
237	        search: &str,
238	        current_focus: Option<&Key>,
239	        collection: &C,
240	        locale: &Locale,
241	        disabled_keys: &BTreeSet<Key>,
242	        disabled_behavior: DisabledBehavior,
243	    ) -> Option<Key> {
244	        // CaseMapper::new() returns CaseMapperBorrowed<'static> which is Copy —
245	        // no caching needed, can be constructed freely.
246	        let case_mapper = icu::casemap::CaseMapper::new();
247	
248	        let langid = locale.language_identifier();
249	
250	        // Determine single-char mode from the raw input, not the lowercased
251	        // query — one typed character can expand to multiple scalars after case
252	        // mapping (e.g., İ → i + combining dot in non-Turkish locales).
253	        let single_char = search.chars().count() == 1;
254	
255	        let query = case_mapper.lowercase_to_string(search, langid);
256	
257	        // FocusOnly: disabled items are focusable, so include them in the scan.
258	        // Skip: disabled items are excluded from the scan.
259	        let skip_disabled = disabled_behavior == DisabledBehavior::Skip;
260	
261	        let all_item_keys = collection
262	            .nodes()
263	            .filter(|n| n.is_focusable() && (!skip_disabled || !disabled_keys.contains(&n.key)))
264	            .map(|n| n.key.clone())
265	            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
266	
267	        if all_item_keys.is_empty() {
268	            return None;
269	        }
270	
271	        // Single-char: start AFTER current_focus (cycling to next match).
272	        // Multi-char: start AT current_focus (refining keeps current match viable).
273	        let start_pos = current_focus
274	            .and_then(|k| all_item_keys.iter().position(|ik| ik == k))
275	            .map_or(0, |p| {
276	                if single_char {
277	                    (p + 1) % all_item_keys.len()
278	                } else {
279	                    p
280	                }
281	            });
282	
283	        // Scan forward only; single-char wraps, multi-char does not.
284	        let scan_len = if single_char {
285	            all_item_keys.len()
286	        } else {
287	            all_item_keys.len().saturating_sub(start_pos)
288	        };
289	
290	        for offset in 0..scan_len {
291	            let idx = (start_pos + offset) % all_item_keys.len();
292	
293	            let key = &all_item_keys[idx];
294	
295	            if let Some(text) = collection.text_value_of(key)
296	                && case_mapper
297	                    .lowercase_to_string(text, langid)
298	                    .starts_with(query.as_ref())
299	            {
300	                return Some(key.clone());
301	            }
302	        }
303	
304	        None
305	    }
306	
307	    /// Reset the type-ahead state (e.g., when the user presses Escape or
308	    /// the component loses focus).
309	    #[must_use]
```

234	#### 4.1.2 For stateless components
235	
236	**§1.1 Props** — Same requirements as stateful §1.4 Props.
237	
238	**§1.2 Connect / API** — A Part enum and stateless `Api` struct created
239	directly from Props (no state/context). Returns `AttrMap` for each part via
240	`ConnectApi`.
241	
242	The `Api` struct stores the full `Props` (`pub struct Api { props: Props }`),
243	not a hand-picked subset of fields. This keeps every stateless component on
244	the same shape, lets `Api::new` be a `const fn` (which extraction-style
245	storage typically cannot when fields like `String id` need to be dropped at
246	end-of-fn), and makes `Api` self-sufficient — adapters can read every Props
247	field through `Api` accessors without retaining `Props` separately. Provide
248	one accessor method per Props field (`id()`, plus one per component-specific
249	field) so the `Api` is a complete adapter-facing view of the component.
250	
251	### 4.2 Anatomy
252	
253	REQUIRED for all tiers. The component's `Part` enum (defined in §1.Y Connect / API) is the single source of truth for the anatomy. The Anatomy section documents the visual nesting structure and element mapping.
254	
255	Contains:
256	
257	1. **ASCII diagram** showing the part nesting hierarchy with role annotations. Part names match the Part enum variant names in kebab-case:
258	
259	    ```text
260	    ComponentName
261	    ├── Root                (required)
262	    ├── Label               (required)
263	    ├── Control        [A]  (required — role="checkbox")
264	    ├── Indicator           (optional — aria-hidden)
265	    └── HiddenInput         (required — native form submission)
266	    ```
267	
268	2. **Parts table** listing each part's element and key attributes. The `data-ars-scope` and `data-ars-part` attributes are set automatically via `ComponentPart::data_attrs()`:
269	
270	| Part    | Element | Key Attributes                                    |
271	| ------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
272	| Root    | `<div>` | `data-ars-state`                                  |
273	| Control | `<div>` | `role="checkbox"`, `aria-checked`, `tabindex="0"` |
274	
275	Parts must be marked as required (component broken without it) or optional (enhances UX, degrades gracefully).
276	
277	### 4.3 Accessibility
278	
279	REQUIRED for all tiers. Subsections:
280	
281	**§3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties** — REQUIRED. A table mapping each interactive part to its ARIA role and required `aria-*` attributes. Reference the `AriaRole` and `AriaAttr` enums from `03-accessibility.md` §2.
282	
283	**§3.2 Keyboard Interaction** — CONDITIONAL (include for any keyboard-interactive component). A table of key bindings following WAI-ARIA Authoring Practices. Must document RTL arrow key reversal where applicable (reference `03-accessibility.md` §4.1 RTL matrix):
284	
285	| Key   | Action               |
286	| ----- | -------------------- |
287	| Space | Toggle checked state |
288	| Tab   | Move focus to/from   |
289	
290	**§3.3 Focus Management** — CONDITIONAL (include when the component manages focus programmatically). Document: initial focus placement, focus trapping (if modal), focus restoration on close, roving tabindex strategy if applicable.
291	
292	**§3.4 Screen Reader Announcements** — CONDITIONAL (include when the component uses live regions for dynamic state changes). Document what is announced, when, and via which live region politeness level.
293	
294	Additional subsections may be added for component-specific concerns (e.g., forced colors mode, virtual cursor containment, disabled element focus policy).
295	
296	### 4.4 Layering (agnostic core ↔ adapter)
297	
298	REQUIRED for **complex** tier. RECOMMENDED for **stateful** and
299	**stateless** tiers when the component has any non-trivial split between
300	agnostic core (`ars-components`) and framework adapters (`ars-leptos`,
301	`ars-dioxus`).
302	
303	The agnostic-core ↔ adapter line is the contract that lets a single
304	state machine drive both Leptos and Dioxus without special-casing
305	either. Spec readers — both human and machine-generated adapters — need
306	to know which types live where so they can reason about reuse,
307	ownership, and `Send + Sync` requirements.
308	
309	The Layering section MUST declare:
310	
311	1. **Agnostic-core types** — `Machine`, `Props`, `Context`, `Event`,
312	   `Effect`, `Api`, `Part`, `Messages`, free functions returning
313	   `AttrMap`, plus any data-only utilities (e.g., `ToastContent`,
314	   `Promise<T, E>`, ZST factories like `Toaster`).
315	2. **Adapter types** — handles holding `Box<dyn Fn(Event)>` or
316	   framework-specific signal types, async runtime glue
317	   (`spawn_local`/`spawn`), DOM/listener wiring (`pointerdown`,
318	   `MutationObserver`, focus traps), and any types whose construction
319	   depends on a `NodeRef`/`MountedData`/web-sys handle.
320	
321	The simplest acceptable form is a two-column table:
322	
323	| Layer           | Type / function                                         |
324	| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
325	| Agnostic core   | `Machine`, `Props`, `Context`, `Event`, `Effect`, `Api` |
326	| Leptos / Dioxus | `use_machine_*`, `RegionRef`, `pointer_capture`         |
327	
328	For purely-agnostic stateless components (e.g. `VisuallyHidden`,
329	`Separator`), a single sentence is sufficient — _"Everything in this
330	spec lives in `ars-components`; adapters re-export the same types
331	through their preludes."_
332	
333	When a behavior is **deliberately not implemented** in the agnostic
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/plans/let-s-implement-ta[REDACTED_SK].md",
  "content": "# Task #204 — Implement `Highlight` agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nImplement the framework-agnostic `Highlight` utility component\n(`spec/components/utility/highlight.md`) — a stateless utility that splits a\ntext string into highlighted / non-highlighted chunks based on one or more\nsearch queries, with three match strategies (`Contains`, `StartsWith`,\n`Fuzzy`), Unicode-aware case folding via ICU4X `CaseMapper`, and multi-query\noverlap merging.\n\nThe component is consumed by adapters (Leptos, Dioxus) that render each\nreturned chunk as `<mark>` or `<span>`. Agnostic core is the only deliverable\nhere — adapter wrappers are out of scope.\n\nGitHub issue: <https://github.com/anthropics/ars-ui/issues/204> · 3 points ·\nno dependencies.\n\n## Files to create / modify\n\n| Action | Path | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| Create | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/highlight.rs` | Component module: `Props`, `MatchStrategy`, `HighlightChunk`, `highlight_chunks`, `Part`, `Api`, tests. |\n| Modify | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` | Add `pub mod highlight;` entry, alphabetized under existing utility modules. |\n| Create | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/ars_components__utility__highlight__tests__*.snap` | Insta snapshots — generated via `cargo insta review` and committed (label `snapshot-reviewed` on PR). |\n\nNo `Cargo.toml` changes (no new dependencies — `ars-i18n` already pulled in by\n`ars-components` and exposes locale-aware case mapping via\n`ars_i18n::to_lowercase`).\n\n## Public API (from spec §1)\n\n```rust\n// spec/components/utility/highlight.md lines 20–46\npub struct Props {\n    pub query: Vec<String>,\n    pub text: String,\n    pub ignore_case: bool,\n    pub match_strategy: MatchStrategy,\n}\n\npub enum MatchStrategy {\n    Contains,\n    StartsWith,\n    Fuzzy,\n}\n\npub struct HighlightChunk<'a> {\n    pub text: &'a str,\n    pub highlighted: bool,\n}\n\npub fn highlight_chunks<'a>(props: &'a Props, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>>;\n```\n\nDefaults (per spec text):\n\n- `ignore_case: bool` defaults to `true`.\n- `match_strategy: MatchStrategy` defaults to `MatchStrategy::Contains`\n  (most permissive — pure inclusion match, matches Ark UI default).\n- `query` and `text` default to empty vec / empty string.\n\n## Utility-pattern conformance additions\n\nThe Highlight spec §1.2 says it uses a \"function-based API\" instead of the\nstandard `ConnectApi` / `AttrMap` pattern — but the issue's acceptance\ncriteria still require \"snapshot tests cover the component's `connect()` /\n`Api` `AttrMap` output for every anatomy part\", and the spec template\n(`foundation/10-component-spec-template.md` §4.1.2) mandates `Props` / `Part`\n/ `Api` for all stateless components. We satisfy both by adding the standard\nutility shape **alongside** the function:\n\n```rust\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"highlight\"]\npub enum Part {\n    Root,\n}\n\npub struct Api { props: Props }\n\nimpl Api {\n    pub const fn new(props: Props) -> Self;\n    pub const fn props(&self) -> &Props;\n    pub fn query(&self) -> &[String];\n    pub fn text(&self) -> &str;\n    pub const fn ignore_case(&self) -> bool;\n    pub const fn match_strategy(&self) -> MatchStrategy;\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap;                       // Part::Root\n    pub fn chunk_attrs(&self, highlighted: bool) -> AttrMap;   // Chunk (parametric)\n    pub fn chunks<'a>(&'a self, locale: &Locale) -> Vec<HighlightChunk<'a>>;\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api {\n    type Part = Part;\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap { /* Root → root_attrs */ }\n}\n```\n\n`Part::Chunk` is intentionally not a Part variant: the chunk anatomy slot is\nparametric on a runtime boolean (`highlighted`), and `ConnectApi::part_attrs`\ntakes a `Part` by value with no payload. Adapters call `chunk_attrs(bool)`\nper emitted chunk. Doc-comment on `Part` explains this.\n\n`root_attrs()` emits `data-ars-scope=\"highlight\"` + `data-ars-part=\"root\"`\n(via `Part::Root.data_attrs()`). `chunk_attrs(b)` emits\n`data-ars-part=\"highlight-chunk\"` + `data-ars-highlighted=\"true\"|\"false\"`.\n\n## Algorithm — `highlight_chunks`\n\nReuse `ars_i18n::to_lowercase(text, locale)` for Unicode-aware,\nlocale-sensitive normalisation. This is the **same** helper the\nalready-implemented `ars-collections::typeahead` (`typeahead.rs:255,297`) uses\nfor case-insensitive matching, so Turkic locales (`İ`/`i`/`I`/`ı`), German\neszett, Greek final sigma, etc. are already handled by the underlying ICU4X\n`CaseMapper`. See \"Open question — case folding\" below.\n\nPipeline:\n\n1. **Short-circuit empty input.** If `props.query.is_empty()` or every entry\n   is empty, return `vec![HighlightChunk { text: &props.text, highlighted: false }]`.\n   If `props.text.is_empty()`, return an empty `Vec`.\n2. **Build normalised text + byte-mapping** (only when `ignore_case`):\n   - Iterate `props.text.char_indices()`. For each char, take its substring\n     (`text[orig_start..orig_end]`), pass to `ars_i18n::to_lowercase`, and\n     append the lowercased bytes to a `String` while pushing `orig_start`\n     into a parallel `Vec<usize>` once per appended byte. Push a sentinel\n     `text.len()` at the end of the map.\n   - The resulting `byte_map[i]` ≡ original-text byte index of the source\n     char that produced normalised byte `i`. Property:\n     `byte_map[match_end_in_normalised]` is always an original-char boundary\n     (or `text.len()` at the end), because each fold block is emitted\n     contiguously.\n   - When `!ignore_case`, the normalised text **is** the original text and\n     `byte_map` is identity (`0..=text.len()`).\n3. **Collect raw match ranges per query × strategy:**\n   - `Contains`: `normalised_text.match_indices(&normalised_query)`. Map each\n     `(start, _)` to original range `(byte_map[start], byte_map[start + normalised_query.len()])`.\n   - `StartsWith`: if `normalised_text.starts_with(&normalised_query)`, emit\n     a single range `(0, byte_map[normalised_query.len()])`.\n   - `Fuzzy`: iterate normalised text char-by-char, advancing a query-char\n     cursor; each matched character emits one original-char range\n     `(orig_start, orig_end)`. Use `char_indices()` on the **original** text\n     and `to_lowercase` per-char to avoid the byte-map indirection for the\n     1-char case (cleaner + handles Unicode expansion correctly).\n4. **Merge overlapping / adjacent ranges.** Sort by start; sweep merging\n   `(a, b), (c, d)` where `c <= b` into `(a, max(b, d))`. Adjacency-merge\n   (`c == b`) keeps the rendered output minimal — naturally consolidates\n   fuzzy 1-char hits that happen to land next to each other and meets spec\n   §3.1's \"consolidate adjacent highlighted chunks\" recommendation.\n5. **Build chunks.** Walk the merged ranges; emit a non-highlighted\n   `HighlightChunk` for each gap and a highlighted one for each range. Skip\n   zero-length segments so we never emit an empty chunk.\n\n### Edge cases the algorithm must hit\n\n- Empty query / all-empty queries → one non-highlighted chunk (`text`).\n- Empty text → empty vec.\n- Query longer than text → no matches → one non-highlighted chunk.\n- All-matched text → one highlighted chunk.\n- Query at end of text → highlighted tail + no trailing non-highlighted chunk.\n- Multi-query overlapping ranges merged into one chunk.\n- Fuzzy with `query.chars().count() > text.chars().count()` → no matches\n  → one non-highlighted chunk.\n- `ignore_case = false` → byte-exact matching, no normalisation pass.\n\n## Tests (TDD — written before implementation)\n\nAll inline in `#[cfg(test)] mod tests { ... }` per `separator.rs` precedent.\nSnapshot tests use `insta::assert_snapshot!` and live under\n`crates/ars-components/src/utility/snapshots/`.\n\n### Strategy / props matrix (named tests required by issue)\n\n| # | Test name | Coverage |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `contains_strategy_matches_substring` | Single contains match in middle of text → 3 chunks. |\n| 2 | `contains_strategy_finds_all_occurrences` | Repeated query → N+1 alternating chunks. |\n| 3 | `starts_with_strategy_matches_at_start` | Match only at position 0. |\n| 4 | `starts_with_strategy_no_match_in_middle` | Query is in middle of text → single non-highlighted chunk. |\n| 5 | `fuzzy_strategy_matches_in_order_with_gaps` | `\"abc\"` in `\"a_b_c\"` → 5 chunks (3 highlighted, 2 gaps). |\n| 6 | `fuzzy_strategy_no_match_when_chars_missing` | Missing char in query → no fuzzy match. |\n| 7 | `unicode_case_folding_turkic_dotted_i` | `tr` locale: `\"İ\"` query matches `\"i\"` text and vice versa. |\n| 8 | `unicode_case_folding_german_eszett` | `de` locale: `\"ss\"` query matches `\"ß\"` text when `ignore_case`. |\n| 9 | `multi_query_overlapping_ranges_merged` | Two queries `[\"foo\", \"oob\"]` on `\"foobar\"` → merged into single highlighted span `\"foob\"`. |\n| 10 | `empty_query_returns_single_non_highlighted_chunk` | `query: vec![]` → `[Chunk { text, highlighted: false }]`. |\n| 11 | `all_empty_queries_returns_single_non_highlighted_chunk` | `query: vec![\"\".into(), \"\".into()]` → same. |\n| 12 | `ignore_case_true_matches_case_insensitive` | `\"hello\"` query matches `\"HELLO\"` text with `ignore_case = true`. |\n| 13 | `ignore_case_false_matches_case_sensitive` | Same input with `ignore_case = false` → no match. |\n| 14 | `empty_text_returns_empty_vec` | Defensive edge case. |\n| 15 | `query_longer_than_text_returns_single_non_highlighted_chunk` | Defensive edge case. |\n\n### Props / `Api` ergonomic tests (mirror separator.rs §\"Props\" and §\"Api accessors\")\n\n| # | Test name |\n|---|---|\n| 16 | `props_default_is_contains_ignore_case_true` |\n| 17 | `props_clone_and_partial_eq_round_trip` |\n| 18 | `props_debug_impl_non_empty` |\n| 19 | `api_exposes_all_props_fields` |\n| 20 | `api_props_round_trips` |\n| 21 | `part_attrs_root_equals_root_attrs` |\n\n### Snapshot tests (insta)\n\n| # | Test name | Snapshots `connect()` / chunk output |\n|---|---|---|\n| 22 | `highlight_root_snapshot` | `root_attrs()` AttrMap. |\n| 23 | `highlight_chunk_highlighted_snapshot` | `chunk_attrs(true)`. |\n| 24 | `highlight_chunk_not_highlighted_snapshot` | `chunk_attrs(false)`. |\n| 25 | `highlight_chunks_contains_en_snapshot` | `highlight_chunks` output for canonical Contains case. |\n| 26 | `highlight_chunks_starts_with_snapshot` | Starts-with case. |\n| 27 | `highlight_chunks_fuzzy_snapshot` | Fuzzy case. |\n| 28 | `highlight_chunks_multi_query_merged_snapshot` | Two overlapping queries. |\n| 29 | `highlight_chunks_turkic_snapshot` | Turkic case folding. |\n| 30 | `highlight_chunks_german_eszett_snapshot` | German eszett. |\n\nPR must apply the `snapshot-reviewed` label after committing the snapshots\n(per `CLAUDE.md` \"Development Workflow\" item 10).\n\n## Key existing utilities to reuse\n\n| Reusable | Where | How |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ars_i18n::to_lowercase(text, locale)` | `crates/ars-i18n/src/case.rs:70` | Locale-aware lowercase using ICU4X `CaseMapper`; matches `typeahead.rs:255` precedent. |\n| `ars_i18n::Locale` | `crates/ars-i18n/src/locale.rs` | Function parameter. |\n| `ars_core::{AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, HasId, HtmlAttr}` | `crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs` | Standard utility-component scaffolding (same imports as `separator.rs:12`). |\n| Insta snapshot pattern | `crates/ars-components/src/utility/separator.rs:418–444` | `snapshot_attrs` helper + `assert_snapshot!`. |\n\n## Spec conformance notes\n\nThe Highlight spec has two small inconsistencies worth flagging — both\nshould be **fixed in this PR** alongside the implementation per `CLAUDE.md`\n\"Spec/code mismatch resolution\":\n\n1. **`spec/components/utility/highlight.md:65`** — references\n   `CaseMapper::fold_turkic()` / `CaseMapper::fold()`. We use\n   `ars_i18n::to_lowercase` (which wraps `CaseMapper::lowercase_to_string`,\n   handles Turkic via `language_identifier`). The acceptance criterion\n   \"Unicode case folding uses ICU4X CaseMapper, not ASCII-only lowering\" is\n   satisfied — the comment in the spec should be reworded to describe the\n   helper used (or the helper renamed). Proposed edit: replace the explicit\n   ICU4X method names with \"delegate to the locale-aware case helpers in\n   `ars-i18n`, which use ICU4X `CaseMapper` under the hood.\"\n2. **`spec/components/utility/highlight.md:111`** — the adapter example\n   calls `highlight_chunks(&props)` (one arg). Function signature is\n   `(&props, &locale)`. Update the example to pass `&locale`.\n\nBoth are doc-only edits in the same component spec file. No other spec files\ntouch this component.\n\n## Verification (end-to-end)\n\nAfter implementation, run **in this order**:\n\n```bash\n# 1. Focused tests for the new module\ncargo test -p ars-components --lib utility::highlight\n\n# 2. Review and accept snapshots, then re-run (must remain green)\ncargo insta review\ncargo test -p ars-components --lib utility::highlight\n\n# 3. Module-level lints / docs\ncargo clippy -p ars-components --all-features -- -D warnings\ncargo doc -p ars-components --no-deps\n\n# 4. Coverage spot-check on the new module\ncargo llvm-cov test -p ars-components --text -- utility::highlight\n\n# 5. Spec validator picks up frontmatter / manifest consistency\ncargo run -p spec-tool -- validate\n\n# 6. Full pre-PR gate (after user approval)\ncargo xci\n```\n\nPR body MUST include `Closes #204`; PR MUST get the `snapshot-reviewed`\nlabel after `.snap` files land (`gh pr edit <num> --add-label \"snapshot-reviewed\"`).\n\n## Open question — case folding API\n\nBefore implementing, confirm the case-folding approach (see \"Spec conformance\nnotes\" §1):\n\n- **Recommended:** use `ars_i18n::to_lowercase(text, locale)` to match the\n  existing `typeahead.rs` precedent, then fix the spec comment to describe\n  this. Avoids new `ars-i18n` API surface, exact same Unicode/Turkic\n  guarantees, exact same test fixtures as Typeahead.\n- **Alternative:** add `fold_string` / `fold_turkic_string` wrappers to\n  `crates/ars-i18n/src/case.rs` and use those. > AGENT

Let me explain the tradeoffs clearly so we can settle the case-folding question.

## Case-folding tradeoff analysis

Both `lowercase_to_string` and `fold_string` exist in ICU4X 2.x and both pass the issue's acceptance criteria (Turkic İ↔i, German ß↔SS). The substantive differences:

| Concern | `ars_i18n::to_lowercase` (Option A) | `fold_string` / `fold_turkic_string` (Option B) | Direct `icu` import (Option C) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Unicode design intent** | Lowercasing — preserves linguistic meaning, can be locale-tailored. | TR21 case folding — canonical form designed for case-insensitive comparison. Strips contextual variants (e.g., Greek final sigma σ vs ς collapse to σ). | Same as B. |
| **Matches spec wording** | No — spec says `fold` / `fold_turkic`. | Yes — modulo wrapper names. | Yes — literal. |
| **Codebase precedent** | Yes — `ars-collections::typeahead.rs:255,297` already uses `lowercase_to_string` for case-insensitive matching. | New — sets a different precedent for the same problem. | Direct `icu` import is precedented in `ars-collections::typeahead` (uses `icu::casemap::CaseMapper` directly). |
| **API surface change** | None. | ~10 lines + tests in `ars-i18n::case`. | Adds `icu` as a direct dep of `ars-components` (workspace dep, new to this crate — needs your approval per CLAUDE.md). |
| **Behaviour on the issue's named tests** | Identical to B/C — Turkic and German pass. | Identical. | Identical. |
| **Behaviour on edge cases** | For Greek text with final sigma, `to_lowercase("Ος")` → `"ος"` (positionally correct lowercase). | Folds to canonical σ — better for "case-insensitive contains". | Same as B. |

**Clear best choice (my recommendation): Option A — `ars_i18n::to_lowercase`.**

Reasons in priority order:

1. **The acceptance criteria** ("Unicode case folding uses ICU4X CaseMapper, not ASCII-only lowering"; "Turkic locale İ→i, German eszett ß→ss handled correctly") are about *not using ASCII* — both options satisfy them identically.
2. **Strong, recent in-tree precedent.** Typeahead already uses `lowercase_to_string` for the same problem (case-insensitive substring matching). Diverging from it in a sibling utility component would create two answers to "how do we case-fold for matching?" in the codebase.
3. **Smallest blast radius** for a 3-point task. No new public API in `ars-i18n`.
4. **The TR21 advantage is real but small.** It matters when matching across locales that handle final sigma / titlecase forms differently. None of the named tests exercise it.

The spec wording at `highlight.md:65` ("via ICU4X `CaseMapper::fold_turkic()`...") would be reworded to describe the helper used — that's already in the plan under "Spec conformance notes" §1.

Confirm and I'll finalize.
